/* TASK: VOLUME_RECUPERACAO */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 001 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 246 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: VOLUME_RECUPERACAO */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-EEdhFBaUJBm3jy59/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
LIBNAME ZDU '/riscosbv/cobranca/brsouza/reguas/rg_rodas_digital';
%LET Usuario = &Usuario;
%LET Senha = &_PASSWORD;
PROC SQL;
CONNECT TO sybaseiq AS dbcon
(host='pta-iqprd01' server=ptaiqprd db=PTAIQCONT user=&Usuario password=&Senha);

CREATE TABLE VOL_RECUPERADO AS
SELECT  * FROM CONNECTION TO dbcon(

		SELECT
		DATE(A.DtPagamento) AS DtPagamento,
		A.TpEntrada,
		B.cod_asses,
		C.razao_nome,
		COUNT(A.NuContratoGestao) AS QTD_CTT

		FROM DBCONTENCIOSO.TBNEGOCIACAOCONTRATO AS A LEFT JOIN DBA.SAJ_PROCESSO AS B
		ON A.NuContratoGestao = B.NuContratoGestao AND
		A.NuProcesso = B.num_processo

		LEFT JOIN SCC_FIN.SAJ_AGENTE AS C
		ON B.cod_asses = C.cod_agente

		WHERE 1=1
		AND DATE(A.DtPagamento) >= '20230101'
		AND SUBSTRING(CAST(A.NuContratoGestao AS VARCHAR),1,2) IN ('12')
		AND ((qt_dias_atraso +1) + datediff(day,dt_asses,A.DtPagamento))>=16
		AND ((qt_dias_atraso +1) + datediff(day,dt_asses,A.DtPagamento))<=70

		GROUP BY

		DtPagamento,
		A.TpEntrada,
		B.cod_asses,
		C.razao_nome);

DISCONNECT FROM dbcon;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE VOL_RECUPERADO_DU AS

SELECT
A.*,
B.DU

FROM WORK.VOL_RECUPERADO AS A
LEFT JOIN ZDU.BASE_AUX_DU AS B

ON A.DtPagamento = B.DATA

;RUN;

DATA WORK.VOL_RECUPERADO_DU;
SET VOL_RECUPERADO_DU;

    IF MONTH(DtPagamento) < 10 THEN SAFRA = COMPRESS(YEAR(DtPagamento) || '0' || MONTH(DtPagamento));
    ELSE SAFRA = COMPRESS(year(DtPagamento) || MONTH(DtPagamento));

	SAFRA2 = SAFRA * 1

;RUN;
PROC SQL;

CREATE TABLE VOL_SINTETICO_SAFRA AS

SELECT
SAFRA,
SAFRA2,
DU,
SUM(QTD_CTT) AS QTD_CTT_REC

FROM VOL_RECUPERADO_DU

GROUP BY
SAFRA,
SAFRA2,
DU

;RUN;

PROC SQL;

CREATE TABLE VOL_SINTETICO_NPS AS

SELECT
SAFRA,
DU,
SUM(QTD_CONTRATO) AS QTD_CTT_NPS

FROM WORK.VOLUMETRIA_NPS

WHERE ENVIO_NORMALIZADO IN ('RODAS')
AND DIVULGACAO LIKE 'ADMINISTRATIVA%'

GROUP BY
SAFRA,
DU

;RUN;

PROC SQL;

CREATE TABLE BD_REC_VOL AS

SELECT
A.SAFRA2 AS SAFRA,
A.DU,
A.QTD_CTT_REC,
B.QTD_CTT_NPS

FROM WORK.VOL_SINTETICO_SAFRA AS A
LEFT JOIN VOL_SINTETICO_NPS AS B

ON A.SAFRA2 = B.SAFRA
AND A.DU = B.DU

;RUN;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: VOLUME_RECUPERACAO */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 001 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: BASE_ANALISE */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 002 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 246 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: BASE_ANALISE */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-I6C8fBH4hggLRfU3/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
LIBNAME ZNPS  '/riscosbv/cobranca/dpompei/NPS';

	
	
	

	LIBNAME adamor  '/riscosbv/cobranca/adamor';
libname NPSENVIO '/riscosbv/cobranca/csaure/Novo_NPS_recebimento/Arquivos_envio';
libname NPS '/riscosbv/cobranca/csaure/Novo_NPS_recebimento';





		PROC SQL; 
	CREATE TABLE AUX_NPS_NOVA_2023 AS
	SELECT
	NUCONTRATO,
	NUCPF_CNPJ,
	DIAREF,
	SAFRA,
	ENVIO,
	CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW,
	NMASSESSORIA,
	DIVULGACAO,
	NOTA,
	CATEGORIA_NPS,
	CASE WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('PROMOTOR')THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_PROMOTOR,
	CASE WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('NEUTRO')THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_NEUTRO,
	CASE WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('DETRATOR')THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_DETRATOR,
	1 AS CONTADOR,

	RESPOSTA_QUALITATIVA_CLI,
	ATRASO,
	FAIXA_ATRASO,
	BV_COLABORA,
	CLASSIFICACAO,
	PUBLICOFINAL,
	PRODUTO,
	CASE
	          
	          WHEN ENVIO IN('BNDU') THEN "BNDU"
		      WHEN ENVIO IN('CARTAO') THEN "CARTAO"
		      WHEN ENVIO IN('CP') THEN "CP"
		      WHEN ENVIO IN('PILOTO CRC') THEN "PILOTO CRC"
		      WHEN ENVIO IN('PILOTO ACORDO CERTO') THEN "PILOTO ACORDO CERTO"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "VIC"          AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2019,1575,1581,1584,1588,1589,1590,1591,1621) THEN "VIC - TESTE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "VIC"          AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2006,1522,1580,1518,1601,2005,1562,1541,1578,186,204,220,241,258,270,497) THEN "VIC - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 2"      AND PUBLICOFINAL = "Teste" THEN "GRUPO 2 - TESTE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 2"      AND PUBLICOFINAL = "Controle" THEN "GRUPO 2 - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 1"      AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2017,2018,1575,1581,1584,1588,1589,1590,1591,1621) THEN "GRUPO 1 - NOVA_REGUA"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 1"      AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2006,1522,1580,1518,1601,2005,1562,1541,1578,186,204,220,241,258,270,497) THEN "GRUPO 1 - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 3"      AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1582,1583) THEN "GRUPO 3 - TESTE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 3"      AND PUBLICOFINAL = "Teste" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2005,1562,2006) THEN "GRUPO 3 - TESTE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 3"      AND PUBLICOFINAL = "Controle" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2005,1562,2006,1541,1578,186,204,220,241,258,270,497) THEN "GRUPO 3 - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Prop Media"   AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2020,1575,1581,1584,1588,1589,1590,1591,1621) THEN "GRUPO 1a4 - TESTE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Prop Media"   AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2006,1522,1580,1518,1601,2005,1562,1541,1578,186,204,220,241,258,270,497) THEN "GRUPO 1a4 - CONTROLE"

	          
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "1. Prim. PMT"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (186,204,220,258,270,497,1309,1562,1590,1592,1619,1625,2005,2017,1588,1575,1591,1627,2006)                               THEN "GRUPO 7 - PRI_PMT - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "2. Recorrente" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (186,204,220,258,270,497,1306,1518,1562,1619,1625,1627,2005,2006,2017,1628,1590,1588,1621,1575,1581,1584,1591,1628,220,258,1619,497,204,270,186,1590,1575,1591,2018,2017,1628,1625,1562,2005)                    THEN "GRUPO 3 - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "2. Recorrente" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1582,1583,1624)                                                                                             THEN "GRUPO 3 - TESTE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "3. Bom Pag. D" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (0,186,204,220,258,270,1575,1581,1584,1588,1590,1591,1619,1621,2017,497,1625,2006)                      THEN "GRUPO 6 - BP DIGITAL - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "3. Bom Pag. D" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1627,1628)                                                                                                 THEN "GRUPO 6 - BP DIGITAL - TESTE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "4. Bom Pagado" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1,186,204,220,258,270,1619,2006,497,1625,1619,1583,1627,1628,1624,1625)                                                                       THEN "GRUPO 1 - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "4. Bom Pagado" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1575,1581,1584,1588,1590,1591,2017,1621)                                                                    THEN "GRUPO 1 - NOVA_REGUA"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "5. Outros PJ"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (186,204,258,270,497,1625,2006)                                                                         THEN "GRUPO 8 - OUTROS PJ - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "6. Outros PF"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1627,1628)                                                                                                  THEN "GRUPO 9 - OUTROS PF DIGITAL - TESTE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "6. Outros PF"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (0,186,204,220,258,270,1308,1562,1619,1625,2005,2006,497,1588,1575,1584,1591,2017)                                           THEN "GRUPO 9 - OUTROS PF DIGITAL - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "7. Outros PF"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (186,204,220,258,270,497,1309,1518,1562,1619,1625,2005,2006,2017,1590,1588,1621,1575,1581,1584,1591,1627,1628,1624)                               THEN "GRUPO 10 - OUTROS PF - CONTROLE"
			  ELSE "SEM GRUPO" END AS SEGMENTACAO_NEW,

	PAGAMENTO_SITE,
	CANAL,
	case when CANAL='ASSESSORIA' or CANAL='MANUAL ASSESSORIA' then 'ASSESSORIA'
	when CANAL ne '' then 'DIGITAL'
	else 'CARTAO' end as CANAL_MACRO,
	case when DIVULGACAO in ('ATIVA_BOM_PAGADOR', 'ATIVA_BOM_PAGADOR_DIGITAL') then 'ATIVA - BP'
	WHEN DIVULGACAO in (
	'ATIVA_TRADICIONAL', 'ATIVA_RECORRENTE',
	'ATIVA_DEMAISPF_DIGITAL', 'ATIVA_VISAO_UNICA' 'RENEG_ATIVA') or
	( DIVULGACAO in ('SEM ASSESSORIA') and ENVIO not in ('CP','CARTAO')) then 'ATIVA - DEMAIS'
	WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('ADMINISTRATIVA_BOMPAGADOR') THEN 'ADM - BP'
	WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('ADM_DEMAISPF_DIGITAL', 'ADMINISTRATIVA', 'ADMINISTRATIVA_RECORRENTE',
	'ADMINISTRATIVA_RENEG') THEN 'ADM - DEMAIS'
	WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('CARTAO_AMIGAVEL') or
	(DIVULGACAO in ('SEM ASSESSORIA') and ENVIO='CARTAO') THEN 'CA'
	WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('CARTAO_AMIGAVEL_DIG') THEN 'CA - DIG'
	ELSE  'OUTROS(CP,SOLAR,CONSIG)'
	END AS DIVULG_AGRUP,

	CASE
	WHEN NOTA BETWEEN 0 AND 10 THEN 'RESPONDEU'
	ELSE 'NÃO RESPONDEU' END AS FLAG_RESPOSTA,
	input(SUBSTR(put(NUCPF_CNPJ,z11.), 7,1), best.) as DIG_CPF_7,
	CASE
	WHEN ENVIO IN ('CARTAO') THEN 'CARTAO'
	WHEN ENVIO IN ('CP') THEN 'CP'
	ELSE 'RODAS' END AS ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
	PRODUTO

FROM BASE_NPS_2023_bkp


WHERE 1=1

AND ENVIO NOT IN ('BNDU')


;QUIT;







	LIBNAME ZNPS  '/riscosbv/cobranca/dpompei/NPS';

	
	
	

	LIBNAME adamor  '/riscosbv/cobranca/adamor';

	PROC SQL; 
	CREATE TABLE AUX_NPS_NOVA AS
	SELECT
	NUCONTRATO,
	NUCPF_CNPJ,
	DIAREF,
	SAFRA,
	ENVIO,
	CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW,
	NMASSESSORIA,
	DIVULGACAO,
	NOTA,
	CATEGORIA_NPS,
	CASE WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('PROMOTOR')THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_PROMOTOR,
	CASE WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('NEUTRO')THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_NEUTRO,
	CASE WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('DETRATOR')THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_DETRATOR,
	1 AS CONTADOR,

	RESPOSTA_QUALITATIVA_CLI,
	ATRASO,
	FAIXA_ATRASO,
	BV_COLABORA,
	CLASSIFICACAO,
	PUBLICOFINAL,
	PRODUTO,
	CASE
	          
	          WHEN ENVIO IN('BNDU') THEN "BNDU"
		      WHEN ENVIO IN('CARTAO') THEN "CARTAO"
		      WHEN ENVIO IN('CP') THEN "CP"
		      WHEN ENVIO IN('PILOTO CRC') THEN "PILOTO CRC"
		      WHEN ENVIO IN('PILOTO ACORDO CERTO') THEN "PILOTO ACORDO CERTO"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "VIC"          AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2019,1575,1581,1584,1588,1589,1590,1591,1621) THEN "VIC - TESTE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "VIC"          AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2006,1522,1580,1518,1601,2005,1562,1541,1578,186,204,220,241,258,270,497) THEN "VIC - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 2"      AND PUBLICOFINAL = "Teste" THEN "GRUPO 2 - TESTE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 2"      AND PUBLICOFINAL = "Controle" THEN "GRUPO 2 - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 1"      AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2017,2018,1575,1581,1584,1588,1589,1590,1591,1621) THEN "GRUPO 1 - NOVA_REGUA"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 1"      AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2006,1522,1580,1518,1601,2005,1562,1541,1578,186,204,220,241,258,270,497) THEN "GRUPO 1 - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 3"      AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1582,1583) THEN "GRUPO 3 - TESTE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 3"      AND PUBLICOFINAL = "Teste" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2005,1562,2006) THEN "GRUPO 3 - TESTE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 3"      AND PUBLICOFINAL = "Controle" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2005,1562,2006,1541,1578,186,204,220,241,258,270,497) THEN "GRUPO 3 - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Prop Media"   AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2020,1575,1581,1584,1588,1589,1590,1591,1621) THEN "GRUPO 1a4 - TESTE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Prop Media"   AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2006,1522,1580,1518,1601,2005,1562,1541,1578,186,204,220,241,258,270,497) THEN "GRUPO 1a4 - CONTROLE"

	          
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "1. Prim. PMT"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (186,204,220,258,270,497,1309,1562,1590,1592,1619,1625,2005,2017,1588,1575,1591,1627,2006)                               THEN "GRUPO 7 - PRI_PMT - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "2. Recorrente" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (186,204,220,258,270,497,1306,1518,1562,1619,1625,1627,2005,2006,2017,1628,1590,1588,1621,1575,1581,1584,1591,1628,220,258,1619,497,204,270,186,1590,1575,1591,2018,2017,1628,1625,1562,2005)                    THEN "GRUPO 3 - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "2. Recorrente" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1582,1583,1624)                                                                                             THEN "GRUPO 3 - TESTE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "3. Bom Pag. D" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (0,186,204,220,258,270,1575,1581,1584,1588,1590,1591,1619,1621,2017,497,1625,2006)                      THEN "GRUPO 6 - BP DIGITAL - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "3. Bom Pag. D" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1627,1628)                                                                                                 THEN "GRUPO 6 - BP DIGITAL - TESTE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "4. Bom Pagado" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1,186,204,220,258,270,1619,2006,497,1625,1619,1583,1627,1628,1624,1625)                                                                       THEN "GRUPO 1 - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "4. Bom Pagado" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1575,1581,1584,1588,1590,1591,2017,1621)                                                                    THEN "GRUPO 1 - NOVA_REGUA"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "5. Outros PJ"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (186,204,258,270,497,1625,2006)                                                                         THEN "GRUPO 8 - OUTROS PJ - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "6. Outros PF"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1627,1628)                                                                                                  THEN "GRUPO 9 - OUTROS PF DIGITAL - TESTE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "6. Outros PF"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (0,186,204,220,258,270,1308,1562,1619,1625,2005,2006,497,1588,1575,1584,1591,2017)                                           THEN "GRUPO 9 - OUTROS PF DIGITAL - CONTROLE"
	          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "7. Outros PF"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (186,204,220,258,270,497,1309,1518,1562,1619,1625,2005,2006,2017,1590,1588,1621,1575,1581,1584,1591,1627,1628,1624)                               THEN "GRUPO 10 - OUTROS PF - CONTROLE"
			  ELSE "SEM GRUPO" END AS SEGMENTACAO_NEW,

	PAGAMENTO_SITE,
	CANAL,
	case when CANAL='ASSESSORIA' or CANAL='MANUAL ASSESSORIA' then 'ASSESSORIA'
	when CANAL ne '' then 'DIGITAL'
	else 'CARTAO' end as CANAL_MACRO,
	case when DIVULGACAO in ('ATIVA_BOM_PAGADOR', 'ATIVA_BOM_PAGADOR_DIGITAL') then 'ATIVA - BP'
	WHEN DIVULGACAO in (
	'ATIVA_TRADICIONAL', 'ATIVA_RECORRENTE',
	'ATIVA_DEMAISPF_DIGITAL', 'ATIVA_VISAO_UNICA' 'RENEG_ATIVA') or
	( DIVULGACAO in ('SEM ASSESSORIA') and ENVIO not in ('CP','CARTAO')) then 'ATIVA - DEMAIS'
	WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('ADMINISTRATIVA_BOMPAGADOR') THEN 'ADM - BP'
	WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('ADM_DEMAISPF_DIGITAL', 'ADMINISTRATIVA', 'ADMINISTRATIVA_RECORRENTE',
	'ADMINISTRATIVA_RENEG') THEN 'ADM - DEMAIS'
	WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('CARTAO_AMIGAVEL') or
	(DIVULGACAO in ('SEM ASSESSORIA') and ENVIO='CARTAO') THEN 'CA'
	WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('CARTAO_AMIGAVEL_DIG') THEN 'CA - DIG'
	ELSE  'OUTROS(CP,SOLAR,CONSIG)'
	END AS DIVULG_AGRUP,

	CASE
	WHEN NOTA BETWEEN 0 AND 10 THEN 'RESPONDEU'
	ELSE 'NÃO RESPONDEU' END AS FLAG_RESPOSTA,
	input(SUBSTR(put(NUCPF_CNPJ,z11.), 7,1), best.) as DIG_CPF_7,
	CASE
	WHEN ENVIO IN ('CARTAO') THEN 'CARTAO'
	WHEN ENVIO IN ('CP') THEN 'CP'
	ELSE 'RODAS' END AS ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
	PRODUTO,

	DMQUE,
RECORRENTE,
MUDANCA_DE_FAIXA,
DT_RESP_ANT,
CATEGORIA_NPS_ANT

FROM ZNPS.BASE_NPS_ATU


WHERE 1=1

AND ENVIO NOT IN ('BNDU')


;QUIT;






PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE AUX_NPS_1 AS

SELECT * FROM WORK.AUX_NPS_2021_COPIA

UNION ALL
SELECT * FROM AUX_NPS_NOVA

union all
select * from AUX_NPS_NOVA_2023

ORDER BY DIAREF

;RUN;

LIBNAME ZDU '/riscosbv/cobranca/brsouza/reguas/rg_rodas_digital';

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE AUX_NPS_NOVA_2 AS

SELECT DISTINCT A.*,
B.DU

FROM WORK.AUX_NPS_NOVA AS A
LEFT JOIN ZDU.BASE_AUX_DU AS B

ON A.DIAREF = B.DATA

;RUN;

data AUX_NPS_NOVA_3;
	set AUX_NPS_NOVA;

	FORMAT DIVULGACAO_NOVA_2 $35.;

	format NMASSESSORIA2 $35.;

	if NMASSESSORIA = '' and CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW in (2021,2022,2041,2040) then NMASSESSORIA2='CONCILIG';
	else NMASSESSORIA2=NMASSESSORIA;

	if not missing(nota) then DIFF_RESP = intck('day',DT_RESP_ANT ,diaref);

	if missing(DT_RESP_ANT) then FL_SEM_RESP_HIST=1;
	else FL_SEM_RESP_HIST=0;

	if FL_SEM_RESP_HIST <> 1 then DIFF_SEM_RESP = intck('day',datepart(today()),DT_RESP_ANT);

	TAMANHO_CONTRATO=length(NUCONTRATO);

	CONTRATO_S=put(NUCONTRATO,z17.);

	TAMANHO_CONTRATO_S=length(CONTRATO_S);

	if atraso >= 60 then fl_atraso_out=1;
	else fl_atraso_out=0;

	if divulgacao = 'ADMINISTRATIVA_RENEG' THEN DIVULGACAO_NOVA_2 = 'ADMINISTRATIVA';
	ELSE DIVULGACAO_NOVA_2=DIVULGACAO;

run;

proc sort data=CONTRATOS_ERRO_BP_ATRASO_SDUP;
by cd_contrato;
run;

proc sort data=CONTRATOS_ERRO_BP_ATRASO_SDUP nodupkey out=ERRO_BP_CERTO_SDUP;
by cd_contrato;
run;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE AUX_NPS_NOVA_4 AS
SELECT
	A.*,

case when A.NUCONTRATO = B.cd_contrato  then 1 else 0 end as flag_vl_erro
FROM WORK.AUX_NPS_NOVA_3 AS A
LEFT JOIN WORK.ERRO_BP_CERTO_SDUP	AS B ON A.NUCONTRATO = B.cd_contrato
;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE AUX_NPS_NOVA_5 AS
SELECT
	A.*
	,
case when A.NUCONTRATO = B.NUCONTRATO then 1 else 0 end as flag_det_abr25
FROM WORK.AUX_NPS_NOVA_3 AS A
LEFT JOIN WORK.DETRATORES_SDUP_202504	AS B ON A.NUCONTRATO = B.NUCONTRATO

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE AUX_NPS_NOVA_6 AS
SELECT
	A.*,
	CASE WHEN ENVIO = "VISAO UNICA" THEN A.ENVIO
	ELSE B.FASE
	END AS DIVULGACAO_NOVA
	,
B.ASSESSORIA AS NMASSESSORIA_NOVA

FROM AUX_NPS_NOVA_3 AS A
LEFT JOIN work.DE_PARA_DIVULG_NOVO AS B

ON A.CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW = B.ID_ASSESSORIA
ORDER BY
	A.DIAREF
;RUN;

PROC SQL; 
CREATE TABLE AUX_NPS_NOVA_7 AS
SELECT
*,
case when DIVULGACAO_NOVA in ('ATIVA_BOM_PAGADOR', 'ATIVA_BOM_PAGADOR_DIGITAL') then 'ATIVA - BP'
WHEN DIVULGACAO_NOVA in ( 'ATIVA_TRADICIONAL', 'ATIVA_RECORRENTE',
'ATIVA_DEMAISPF_DIGITAL', 'ATIVA_VISAO_UNICA' 'RENEG_ATIVA') or
( DIVULGACAO_NOVA in ('SEM ASSESSORIA') and ENVIO not in ('CP','CARTAO')) then 'ATIVA - DEMAIS'
WHEN DIVULGACAO_NOVA in ('ADMINISTRATIVA_BOMPAGADOR') THEN 'ADM - BP'
WHEN DIVULGACAO_NOVA in ('ADM_DEMAISPF_DIGITAL', 'ADMINISTRATIVA', 'ADMINISTRATIVA_RECORRENTE',
'ADMINISTRATIVA_RENEG') THEN 'ADM - DEMAIS'
WHEN DIVULGACAO_NOVA in ('CARTAO_AMIGAVEL') or
(DIVULGACAO_NOVA in ('SEM ASSESSORIA') and ENVIO='CARTAO') THEN 'CA'
WHEN DIVULGACAO_NOVA in ('CARTAO_AMIGAVEL_DIG') THEN 'CA - DIG'
ELSE  'OUTROS(CP,SOLAR,CONSIG)'
END AS DIVULG_AGRUP_NOVA

FROM AUX_NPS_NOVA_6

;QUIT;



PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE AUX_NPS_NOVA_8 AS
SELECT
	A.*,
b.FASECOB,

case when b.FASECOB='AT01' then '2)1 PMT'
when b.FASECOB='AT02' then '5) Bom Pagador Digital Teste'
when b.FASECOB='AT03' then '6) Bom Pagador Digital Controle'
when b.FASECOB='AT04' then '7) Bom Pagador Teste'
when b.FASECOB='AT05' then '3) Recorrente Teste'
when b.FASECOB='AT06' then '4) Recorrente Controle'
when b.FASECOB='AT07' then '9) Outros PJ'
when b.FASECOB='AT08' then '10) Outros PF Digital Teste'
when b.FASECOB='AT09' then '11) Outros PF Digital Controle'
when b.FASECOB='AT10' then '12) Outros PF'
when b.FASECOB='AT11' then '8) Bom Pagador Controle'
when b.FASECOB='RT10' then '1) Celula Reneg'
ELSE '99) SEM SEG' END AS SEG_REVISADA,
case when PUT(A.NUCONTRATO,z14.) = c.nr_contrato then 1 else 0 end as FL_ERRO_COLL,
c.safra as SAFRA_ERRO_COLL

FROM AUX_NPS_NOVA_7 AS A
LEFT JOIN work.TOTAL_ENVIOS AS B
ON PUT(A.NUCONTRATO,z14.) = B.nr_contrato and a.SAFRA=b.ref_seg

LEFT JOIN work.COLL_ERRO_SDUP AS c

ON PUT(A.NUCONTRATO,z14.) = c.NR_CONTRATO and a.safra >= 202501

;RUN;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE AUX_NPS_NOVA_8 AS
SELECT
	A.*,
b.FASECOB,

case when b.FASECOB='AT01' then '2)1 PMT'
when b.FASECOB='AT02' then '5) Bom Pagador Digital Teste'
when b.FASECOB='AT03' then '6) Bom Pagador Digital Controle'
when b.FASECOB='AT04' then '7) Bom Pagador Teste'
when b.FASECOB='AT05' then '3) Recorrente Teste'
when b.FASECOB='AT06' then '4) Recorrente Controle'
when b.FASECOB='AT07' then '9) Outros PJ'
when b.FASECOB='AT08' then '10) Outros PF Digital Teste'
when b.FASECOB='AT09' then '11) Outros PF Digital Controle'
when b.FASECOB='AT10' then '12) Outros PF'
when b.FASECOB='AT11' then '8) Bom Pagador Controle'
when b.FASECOB='RT10' then '1) Celula Reneg'
ELSE '99) SEM SEG' END AS SEG_REVISADA,
case when A.NUCONTRATO = c.nucontrato then 1 else 0 end as FL_ERRO_COLL,
c.safra as SAFRA_ERRO_COLL

FROM AUX_NPS_NOVA_7 AS A
LEFT JOIN work.TOTAL_ENVIOS AS B
ON PUT(A.NUCONTRATO,z14.) = B.nr_contrato and a.SAFRA=b.ref_seg

LEFT JOIN work.COLL_ERRO_SDUP AS c

ON A.NUCONTRATO = c.nucontrato and a.safra >= 202501

;RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY3_0000 AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.DIVULG_AGRUP_NOVA as DIVULG_AGRUP,
          t1.NMASSESSORIA_NOVA as NMASSESSORIA,
          t1.CANAL_MACRO,
          t1.RESPOSTA_QUALITATIVA_CLI,
          t1.FL_ERRO_COLL,
          t1.FAIXA_ATRASO,
          t1.PRODUTO,
		  t1.CATEGORIA_NPS,
		  t1.CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW,
		  t1.DIVULGACAO_NOVA,
		  t1.SEG_REVISADA,
		  t1.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
          t1.FLAG_RESPOSTA,
          
            (COUNT(t1.NUCONTRATO)) AS COUNT_of_NUCONTRATO,
          
            (SUM(t1.FLAG_PROMOTOR)) AS SUM_of_FLAG_PROMOTOR,
          
            (SUM(t1.FLAG_NEUTRO)) AS SUM_of_FLAG_NEUTRO,
          
            (SUM(t1.FLAG_DETRATOR)) AS SUM_of_FLAG_DETRATOR,
          
            (SUM(t1.CONTADOR)) AS SUM_of_CONTADOR
      FROM WORK.AUX_NPS_NOVA_8 t1
      GROUP BY t1.SAFRA,
               t1.DIVULG_AGRUP_NOVA,
               t1.NMASSESSORIA_NOVA,
               t1.CANAL_MACRO,
               t1.RESPOSTA_QUALITATIVA_CLI,
               t1.FL_ERRO_COLL,
               t1.FAIXA_ATRASO,
               t1.PRODUTO,
			   t1.CATEGORIA_NPS,
			   t1.CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW,
			   t1.DIVULGACAO_NOVA,
			   t1.SEG_REVISADA,
			   t1.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
               t1.FLAG_RESPOSTA;
QUIT;

LIBNAME ZNPS  '/riscosbv/cobranca/dpompei/NPS';

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE NPS AS

SELECT DISTINCT
NUCONTRATO,
NUCPF_CNPJ,
NOME,
DIAREF,
SAFRA,

CASE
WHEN ENVIO IN ('CARTAO') THEN 'CARTAO'
WHEN ENVIO IN ('CP') THEN 'CP'
ELSE 'RODAS' END AS ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
CATEGORIA_NPS,
CASE WHEN NOTA BETWEEN 0 AND 10 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_RESPOSTA

FROM ZNPS.BASE_NPS_ATU

WHERE 1=1
AND ENVIO NOT IN ('BNDU')

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;

CREATE TABLE DETRATOR_CONTAGEM AS

SELECT DISTINCT
A.NUCONTRATO,

A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
COUNT(B.NUCONTRATO) AS QTD_DETRATOR,
MAX(B.DIAREF) AS MAXDT_DETRATOR FORMAT=DDMMYY10.

FROM WORK.NPS AS A
LEFT JOIN ZNPS.BASE_NPS_ATU AS B

ON A.NUCONTRATO = B.NUCONTRATO

WHERE B.CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('DETRATOR')

GROUP BY
A.NUCONTRATO,

A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO

;RUN;
PROC SQL;

CREATE TABLE NEUTRO_CONTAGEM AS

SELECT DISTINCT
A.NUCONTRATO,

A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
COUNT(B.NUCONTRATO) AS QTD_NEUTRO,
MAX(B.DIAREF) AS MAXDT_NEUTRO FORMAT=DDMMYY10.

FROM WORK.NPS AS A
LEFT JOIN ZNPS.BASE_NPS_ATU AS B

ON A.NUCONTRATO = B.NUCONTRATO

WHERE B.CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('NEUTRO')

GROUP BY
A.NUCONTRATO,

A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO

;RUN;

PROC SQL;

CREATE TABLE PROMOTOR_CONTAGEM AS

SELECT DISTINCT
A.NUCONTRATO,

A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
COUNT(B.NUCONTRATO) AS QTD_PROMOTOR,
MAX(B.DIAREF) AS MAXDT_PROMOTOR FORMAT=DDMMYY10.

FROM WORK.NPS AS A
LEFT JOIN ZNPS.BASE_NPS_ATU AS B

ON A.NUCONTRATO = B.NUCONTRATO

WHERE B.CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('PROMOTOR')

GROUP BY
A.NUCONTRATO,

A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO

;RUN;
PROC SQL;

CREATE TABLE SEMRESP_CONTAGEM AS

SELECT DISTINCT
A.NUCONTRATO,

A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
COUNT(B.NUCONTRATO) AS QTD_SEMRESP,
MAX(B.DIAREF) AS MAXDT_SEMRESP FORMAT=DDMMYY10.

FROM WORK.NPS AS A
LEFT JOIN ZNPS.BASE_NPS_ATU AS B

ON A.NUCONTRATO = B.NUCONTRATO

WHERE B.CATEGORIA_NPS IS NULL

GROUP BY
A.NUCONTRATO,

A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO

;RUN;
PROC SQL;

CREATE TABLE CONTAGEM_TOTAL_RESPOSTAS AS

SELECT DISTINCT
A.NUCONTRATO,

A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
COUNT(B.NUCONTRATO) AS QTD_RESPOSTAS

FROM WORK.NPS AS A
LEFT JOIN ZNPS.BASE_NPS_ATU AS B

ON A.NUCONTRATO = B.NUCONTRATO

WHERE B.NOTA IS NOT NULL

GROUP BY
A.NUCONTRATO,

A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO

;RUN;
PROC SQL;

CREATE TABLE CONTAGEM_TOTAL_PESQUISAS AS

SELECT DISTINCT
A.NUCONTRATO,

A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
COUNT(B.NUCONTRATO) AS QTD_PESQUISAS

FROM WORK.NPS AS A
LEFT JOIN ZNPS.BASE_NPS_ATU AS B

ON A.NUCONTRATO = B.NUCONTRATO

GROUP BY
A.NUCONTRATO,

A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO

;RUN;

PROC SQL;

CREATE TABLE ANALISE_HISTORICO AS

SELECT DISTINCT
A.NUCONTRATO,
A.NUCPF_CNPJ,
A.NOME,

A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
B.QTD_PROMOTOR,
B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR,
C.QTD_NEUTRO,
C.MAXDT_NEUTRO,
D.QTD_DETRATOR,
D.MAXDT_DETRATOR,
E.QTD_SEMRESP,
E.MAXDT_SEMRESP,

CASE

WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL THEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL THEN C.MAXDT_NEUTRO
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL THEN D.MAXDT_DETRATOR

WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR > C.MAXDT_NEUTRO THEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR > D.MAXDT_DETRATOR THEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO > B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR THEN C.MAXDT_NEUTRO
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO > D.MAXDT_DETRATOR THEN C.MAXDT_NEUTRO
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR > B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR THEN D.MAXDT_DETRATOR
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR > C.MAXDT_NEUTRO THEN D.MAXDT_DETRATOR

WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR > C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR > D.MAXDT_DETRATOR  THEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO > B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO > D.MAXDT_DETRATOR  THEN C.MAXDT_NEUTRO
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR > B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR > C.MAXDT_NEUTRO  THEN D.MAXDT_DETRATOR
END AS DT_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA FORMAT=DDMMYY10.,

CASE
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR > C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR > D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR > E.MAXDT_SEMRESP  THEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR
WHEN C.MAXDT_NEUTRO > B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO > D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO > E.MAXDT_SEMRESP THEN C.MAXDT_NEUTRO
WHEN D.MAXDT_DETRATOR > B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR > C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR > E.MAXDT_SEMRESP THEN D.MAXDT_DETRATOR
WHEN E.MAXDT_SEMRESP > B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP > C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP > D.MAXDT_DETRATOR THEN E.MAXDT_SEMRESP
END AS DT_ULTIMA_PESQUISA FORMAT=DDMMYY10.,

CASE

WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL THEN 'NUNCA RESPONDEU'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NULL THEN 'PROMOTOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NULL THEN 'NEUTRO'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NULL THEN 'DETRATOR'

WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR > E.MAXDT_SEMRESP  THEN 'SEM RESPOSTA > PROMOTOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO > E.MAXDT_SEMRESP  THEN 'SEM RESPOSTA > NEUTRO'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR > E.MAXDT_SEMRESP  THEN 'SEM RESPOSTA > DETRATOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP  THEN 'PROMOTOR > SEM RESPOSTA'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP  THEN 'NEUTRO > SEM RESPOSTA'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP  THEN 'DETRATOR > SEM RESPOSTA'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR > C.MAXDT_NEUTRO  THEN 'NEUTRO > PROMOTOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO  THEN 'PROMOTOR > NEUTRO'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR > D.MAXDT_DETRATOR  THEN 'DETRATOR > PROMOTOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR  THEN 'PROMOTOR > DETRATOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO > D.MAXDT_DETRATOR  THEN 'DETRATOR > NEUTRO'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR  THEN 'NEUTRO > DETRATOR'

WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR > E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO THEN 'SEM RESPOSTA > PROMOTOR > NEUTRO'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR > E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR THEN 'SEM RESPOSTA > PROMOTOR > DETRATOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO > E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR THEN 'SEM RESPOSTA > NEUTRO > PROMOTOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO > E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR THEN 'SEM RESPOSTA > NEUTRO > DETRATOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR > E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR THEN 'SEM RESPOSTA > DETRATOR > PROMOTOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR > E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO THEN 'SEM RESPOSTA > DETRATOR > NEUTRO'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR > B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP THEN 'PROMOTOR > DETRATOR > SEM RESPOSTA'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP > C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR THEN 'NEUTRO > SEM RESPOSTA > PROMOTOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP > C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR THEN 'NEUTRO > SEM RESPOSTA > DETRATOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR > C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP THEN 'NEUTRO > PROMOTOR > SEM RESPOSTA'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR > C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP THEN 'NEUTRO > DETRATOR > SEM RESPOSTA'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP > D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR THEN 'DETRATOR > SEM RESPOSTA > PROMOTOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP > D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO THEN 'DETRATOR > SEM RESPOSTA > NEUTRO'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR > D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP THEN 'DETRATOR > PROMOTOR > SEM RESPOSTA'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO > D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP THEN 'DETRATOR > NEUTRO > SEM RESPOSTA'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP > B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO THEN 'PROMOTOR > SEM RESPOSTA > NEUTRO'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP > B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR THEN 'PROMOTOR > SEM RESPOSTA > DETRATOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO > B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP THEN 'PROMOTOR > NEUTRO > SEM RESPOSTA'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO > B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR THEN 'PROMOTOR > NEUTRO > DETRATOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR > B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO THEN 'PROMOTOR > DETRATOR > NEUTRO'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR > C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR THEN 'NEUTRO > PROMOTOR > DETRATOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR > C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR THEN 'NEUTRO > DETRATOR > PROMOTOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR > D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO THEN 'DETRATOR > PROMOTOR > NEUTRO'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO > D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR THEN 'DETRATOR > NEUTRO > PROMOTOR'

WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR THEN 'SEM RESPOSTA > PROMOTOR > NEUTRO > DETRATOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO THEN 'SEM RESPOSTA > PROMOTOR > DETRATOR > NEUTRO'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR THEN 'SEM RESPOSTA > NEUTRO > PROMOTOR > DETRATOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR THEN 'SEM RESPOSTA > NEUTRO > DETRATOR > PROMOTOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO THEN 'SEM RESPOSTA > DETRATOR > PROMOTOR > NEUTRO'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR THEN 'SEM RESPOSTA > DETRATOR > NEUTRO > PROMOTOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR THEN 'PROMOTOR > SEM RESPOSTA > NEUTRO > DETRATOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO THEN 'PROMOTOR > SEM RESPOSTA > DETRATOR > NEUTRO'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR THEN 'PROMOTOR > NEUTRO > SEM RESPOSTA > DETRATOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP THEN 'PROMOTOR > NEUTRO > DETRATOR > SEM RESPOSTA'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO THEN 'PROMOTOR > DETRATOR > SEM RESPOSTA > NEUTRO'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP THEN 'PROMOTOR > DETRATOR > NEUTRO > SEM RESPOSTA'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR THEN 'NEUTRO > SEM RESPOSTA > PROMOTOR > DETRATOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR THEN 'NEUTRO > SEM RESPOSTA > DETRATOR > PROMOTOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR THEN 'NEUTRO > PROMOTOR > SEM RESPOSTA > DETRATOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP THEN 'NEUTRO > PROMOTOR > DETRATOR > SEM RESPOSTA'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR THEN 'NEUTRO > DETRATOR > SEM RESPOSTA > PROMOTOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < D.MAXDT_DETRATOR AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP THEN 'NEUTRO > DETRATOR > PROMOTOR > SEM RESPOSTA'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO THEN 'DETRATOR > SEM RESPOSTA > PROMOTOR > NEUTRO'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR THEN 'DETRATOR > SEM RESPOSTA > NEUTRO > PROMOTOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO THEN 'DETRATOR > PROMOTOR > SEM RESPOSTA > NEUTRO'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP THEN 'DETRATOR > PROMOTOR > NEUTRO > SEM RESPOSTA'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR THEN 'DETRATOR > NEUTRO > SEM RESPOSTA > PROMOTOR'
WHEN B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR IS NOT NULL AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR IS NOT NULL AND E.MAXDT_SEMRESP IS NOT NULL AND D.MAXDT_DETRATOR < C.MAXDT_NEUTRO AND C.MAXDT_NEUTRO < B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR AND B.MAXDT_PROMOTOR < E.MAXDT_SEMRESP THEN 'DETRATOR > NEUTRO > PROMOTOR > SEM RESPOSTA'
ELSE 'VERIFICAR' END AS JORNADA_NPS,

F.QTD_RESPOSTAS,
G.QTD_PESQUISAS

FROM WORK.NPS AS A
LEFT JOIN WORK.PROMOTOR_CONTAGEM AS B
ON A.NUCONTRATO = B.NUCONTRATO

AND A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO = B.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO

LEFT JOIN WORK.NEUTRO_CONTAGEM AS C
ON A.NUCONTRATO = C.NUCONTRATO

AND A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO = C.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO

LEFT JOIN WORK.DETRATOR_CONTAGEM AS D
ON A.NUCONTRATO = D.NUCONTRATO

AND A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO = D.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO

LEFT JOIN WORK.SEMRESP_CONTAGEM AS E
ON A.NUCONTRATO = E.NUCONTRATO

AND A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO = E.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO

LEFT JOIN WORK.CONTAGEM_TOTAL_RESPOSTAS AS F
ON A.NUCONTRATO = F.NUCONTRATO

AND A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO = F.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO

LEFT JOIN WORK.CONTAGEM_TOTAL_PESQUISAS AS G
ON A.NUCONTRATO = G.NUCONTRATO

AND A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO = G.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO

WHERE F.QTD_RESPOSTAS IS NOT NULL

;RUN;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE SINTETICO_JORNADA_RESP AS

SELECT DISTINCT

ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
MAXDT_PROMOTOR,
MAXDT_NEUTRO,
MAXDT_DETRATOR,
MAXDT_SEMRESP,
DT_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA,
DT_ULTIMA_PESQUISA,
JORNADA_NPS,
SUM(QTD_PROMOTOR) AS QTD_PROMOTOR,
SUM(QTD_NEUTRO) AS QTD_NEUTRO,
SUM(QTD_DETRATOR) AS QTD_DETRATOR,
SUM(QTD_SEMRESP) AS QTD_SEMRESP,
SUM(QTD_RESPOSTAS) AS QTD_RESPOSTAS,
SUM(QTD_PESQUISAS) AS QTD_PESQUISAS,
COUNT(NUCONTRATO) AS QTD_CONTRATO

FROM ANALISE_HISTORICO

GROUP BY

ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
MAXDT_PROMOTOR,
MAXDT_NEUTRO,
MAXDT_DETRATOR,
MAXDT_SEMRESP,
DT_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA,
DT_ULTIMA_PESQUISA,
JORNADA_NPS

;RUN;

DATA WORK.ANALISE_HISTORICO;
SET ANALISE_HISTORICO;

DIAS_SEM_RESPOSTA = INTCK('day',DT_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA, DT_ULTIMA_PESQUISA);

IF MONTH(DT_ULTIMA_PESQUISA) < 10 THEN SAFRA_ULTIMA_PESQUISA = COMPRESS(YEAR(DT_ULTIMA_PESQUISA) || '0' || MONTH(DT_ULTIMA_PESQUISA));
    ELSE SAFRA_ULTIMA_PESQUISA = COMPRESS(year(DT_ULTIMA_PESQUISA) || MONTH(DT_ULTIMA_PESQUISA));

IF MONTH(DT_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA) < 10 THEN SAFRA_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA = COMPRESS(YEAR(DT_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA) || '0' || MONTH(DT_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA));
    ELSE SAFRA_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA = COMPRESS(year(DT_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA) || MONTH(DT_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA));

IF MONTH(TODAY()) < 10 THEN SAFRA_ATUAL = COMPRESS(YEAR(TODAY()) || '0' || MONTH(TODAY()));
    ELSE SAFRA_ATUAL = COMPRESS(year(TODAY()) || MONTH(TODAY()))

;RUN;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE SINTETICO_JORNADA_2 AS

SELECT

*,
CASE
WHEN DIAS_SEM_RESPOSTA = 0 THEN '1 | 0 DIAS'
WHEN DIAS_SEM_RESPOSTA BETWEEN 1 AND 45 THEN '2 | 1 A 45 DIAS'
WHEN DIAS_SEM_RESPOSTA BETWEEN 46 AND 90 THEN '3 | 46 A 90 DIAS'
WHEN DIAS_SEM_RESPOSTA BETWEEN 91 AND 180 THEN '4 | 91 A 180 DIAS'
WHEN DIAS_SEM_RESPOSTA BETWEEN 180 AND 365 THEN '5 | 180 A 365 DIAS'
WHEN DIAS_SEM_RESPOSTA >365 THEN '6 | > 365 DIAS'
WHEN JORNADA_NPS IN ('NUNCA RESPONDEU') THEN '7 | NUNCA RESPONDEU'
ELSE '7 | NUNCA RESPONDEU' END AS FAIXA_DIAS_SEMRESP,

CASE
WHEN DT_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA IS NULL THEN 'NUNCA RESPONDEU'
WHEN DT_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA IS NOT NULL AND SAFRA_ULTIMA_PESQUISA > SAFRA_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA THEN 'ÚLTIMA RESPOSTA EM SAFRAS ANTERIORES'
WHEN DT_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA IS NOT NULL AND SAFRA_ULTIMA_PESQUISA = SAFRA_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA THEN 'ÚLTIMA RESPOSTA NA ÚLTIMA PESQUISA'
ELSE 'VERIFICAR' END AS CONDENSADO_JORNADA,

CASE
WHEN DT_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA IS NULL THEN 'NUNCA RESPONDEU'
ELSE 'JÁ RESPONDEU' END AS FILTRO_RESPOSTA

FROM ANALISE_HISTORICO

;RUN;
DATA WORK.SINTETICO_JORNADA_2;
SET SINTETICO_JORNADA_2;

CPF_TXT = PUT(NUCPF_CNPJ, Z11.)

;RUN;

PROC SQL;

A.*,
B.idBV

FROM SINTETICO_JORNADA_2 AS A
LEFT JOIN HASH.TBPESSOA AS B

ON A.CPF_TXT = B.idpessoa

;RUN;

. as IdBv,

PROC SQL; 
CREATE TABLE VOLUMETRIA_NPS_1 AS
SELECT
SAFRA,
DIAREF,
FLAG_TELEFONE,
UF,













CASE
WHEN ENVIO IN ('CARTAO') THEN 'CARTAO'
WHEN ENVIO IN ('CP') THEN 'CP'
ELSE 'RODAS' END AS ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,

CASE
WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('PROMOTOR') THEN COUNT(NUCONTRATO) ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_PROMOTOR,
CASE
WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('NEUTRO') THEN COUNT(NUCONTRATO) ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_NEUTRO,
CASE
WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('DETRATOR') THEN COUNT(NUCONTRATO) ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_DETRATOR,

DIVULGACAO,
NMASSESSORIA,
CATEGORIA_NPS,
COUNT(NUCONTRATO) AS QTD_CONTRATO,
CASE
WHEN NOTA BETWEEN 0 AND 10 THEN 'RESPONDEU'
ELSE 'NÃO RESPONDEU' END AS FLAG_RESPOSTA

FROM ZNPS.BASE_NPS_ATU

WHERE 1=1
AND ENVIO NOT IN ('BNDU')

GROUP BY
SAFRA,
DIAREF,
FLAG_TELEFONE,
UF,
ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
DIVULGACAO,
NMASSESSORIA,
CATEGORIA_NPS,
FLAG_RESPOSTA

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE VOLUMETRIA_NPS AS

SELECT DISTINCT A.*,
B.DU

FROM WORK.VOLUMETRIA_NPS_1 AS A
LEFT JOIN ZDU.BASE_AUX_DU AS B

ON A.DIAREF = B.DATA

;RUN;

LIBNAME ZNPS  '/riscosbv/cobranca/dpompei/NPS';
LIBNAME ZDU '/riscosbv/cobranca/brsouza/reguas/rg_rodas_digital';

PROC SQL; 
CREATE TABLE VOLUMETRIA_NPS_12 AS
SELECT
SAFRA,
DIAREF,
RESPOSTA_QUALITATIVA_CLI,


case when DIVULGACAO in ('ATIVA_BOM_PAGADOR') then 'ATIVA - BP'
WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('ATIVA_BOM_PAGADOR_DIGITAL',
'ATIVA_TRADICIONAL', 'ATIVA_RECORRENTE',
'ATIVA_DEMAISPF_DIGITAL', 'ATIVA_VISAO_UNICA' 'RENEG_ATIVA') or
( DIVULGACAO in ('SEM ASSESSORIA') and ENVIO not in ('CP','CARTAO')) then 'ATIVA - DEMAIS'
WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('ADMINISTRATIVA_BOMPAGADOR') THEN 'ADM - BP'
WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('ADM_DEMAISPF_DIGITAL', 'ADMINISTRATIVA', 'ADMINISTRATIVA_RECORRENTE',
'ADMINISTRATIVA_RENEG') THEN 'ADM - DEMAIS'
WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('CARTAO_AMIGAVEL') or
(DIVULGACAO in ('SEM ASSESSORIA') and ENVIO='CARTAO') THEN 'CA'
WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('CARTAO_AMIGAVEL_DIG') THEN 'CA - DIG'
ELSE  'OUTROS(CP,SOLAR,CONSIG)'
END AS DIVULG_AGRUP,

CASE
WHEN ENVIO IN ('CARTAO') THEN 'CARTAO'
WHEN ENVIO IN ('CP') THEN 'CP'
ELSE 'RODAS' END AS ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,

CASE
WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('PROMOTOR') THEN COUNT(NUCONTRATO) ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_PROMOTOR,
CASE
WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('NEUTRO') THEN COUNT(NUCONTRATO) ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_NEUTRO,
CASE
WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('DETRATOR') THEN COUNT(NUCONTRATO) ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_DETRATOR,

DIVULGACAO,
NMASSESSORIA,
CATEGORIA_NPS,
COUNT(NUCONTRATO) AS QTD_CONTRATO,
CASE
WHEN NOTA BETWEEN 0 AND 10 THEN 'RESPONDEU'
ELSE 'NÃO RESPONDEU' END AS FLAG_RESPOSTA,
CANAL,
case when CANAL='ASSESSORIA' or CANAL='MANUAL ASSESSORIA' then 'ASSESSORIA'
when CANAL='SAC' then 'SAC'
when CANAL ne '' then 'DIGITAL'
else 'SEM CANAL' end as CANAL_MACRO,
PRODUTO

FROM ZNPS.BASE_NPS_ATU

WHERE 1=1
AND ENVIO NOT IN ('BNDU')

GROUP BY
SAFRA,
DIAREF,
RESPOSTA_QUALITATIVA_CLI,
DIVULG_AGRUP,
ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
DIVULGACAO,
NMASSESSORIA,
CATEGORIA_NPS,
FLAG_RESPOSTA,
CANAL,
CANAL_MACRO,
PRODUTO

;QUIT;

    ELSE SAFRA = COMPRESS(year(DT_ENVIO) || MONTH(DT_ENVIO))

/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-ilRWxgKDIUhhEJli/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */

LIBNAME ZNPS  '/riscosbv/cobranca/dpompei/NPS';

PROC SQL; 
CREATE TABLE AUX_NPS_NOVA AS
SELECT
NUCONTRATO,
NUCPF_CNPJ,
DIAREF,
SAFRA,
ENVIO,
CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW,
NMASSESSORIA,
DIVULGACAO,
NOTA,
CATEGORIA_NPS,
CASE WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('PROMOTOR')THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_PROMOTOR,
CASE WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('NEUTRO')THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_NEUTRO,
CASE WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('DETRATOR')THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_DETRATOR,
1 AS CONTADOR,
RESPOSTA_QUALITATIVA_CLI,
ATRASO,
case when atraso between 60 and 70 then 1 else 0 end as fl_pre_cont,
FAIXA_ATRASO,
DMQUE,
SCORE3,
CLASSIFICACAO,
PUBLICOFINAL,
CASE
WHEN NOTA BETWEEN 0 AND 10 THEN 'RESPONDEU'
ELSE 'NÃO RESPONDEU' END AS FLAG_RESPOSTA,
CASE
          
          WHEN ENVIO IN('BNDU') THEN "BNDU"
	      WHEN ENVIO IN('CARTAO') THEN "CARTAO"
	      WHEN ENVIO IN('CP') THEN "CP"
	      WHEN ENVIO IN('PILOTO CRC') THEN "PILOTO CRC"
	      WHEN ENVIO IN('PILOTO ACORDO CERTO') THEN "PILOTO ACORDO CERTO"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "VIC"          AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2019,1575,1581,1584,1588,1589,1590,1591,1621) THEN "VIC - TESTE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "VIC"          AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2006,1522,1580,1518,1601,2005,1562,1541,1578,186,204,220,241,258,270,497) THEN "VIC - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 2"      AND PUBLICOFINAL = "Teste" THEN "GRUPO 2 - TESTE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 2"      AND PUBLICOFINAL = "Controle" THEN "GRUPO 2 - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 1"      AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2017,2018,1575,1581,1584,1588,1589,1590,1591,1621) THEN "GRUPO 1 - NOVA_REGUA"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 1"      AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2006,1522,1580,1518,1601,2005,1562,1541,1578,186,204,220,241,258,270,497) THEN "GRUPO 1 - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 3"      AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1582,1583) THEN "GRUPO 3 - TESTE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 3"      AND PUBLICOFINAL = "Teste" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2005,1562,2006) THEN "GRUPO 3 - TESTE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 3"      AND PUBLICOFINAL = "Controle" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2005,1562,2006,1541,1578,186,204,220,241,258,270,497) THEN "GRUPO 3 - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Prop Media"   AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2020,1575,1581,1584,1588,1589,1590,1591,1621) THEN "GRUPO 1a4 - TESTE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Prop Media"   AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2006,1522,1580,1518,1601,2005,1562,1541,1578,186,204,220,241,258,270,497) THEN "GRUPO 1a4 - CONTROLE"

          
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "1. Prim. PMT"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (186,204,220,258,270,497,1309,1562,1590,1592,1619,1625,2005,2017,1588,1575,1591,1627,2006)                               THEN "GRUPO 7 - PRI_PMT - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "2. Recorrente" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (186,204,220,258,270,497,1306,1518,1562,1619,1625,1627,2005,2006,2017,1628,1590,1588,1621,1575,1581,1584,1591,1628,220,258,1619,497,204,270,186,1590,1575,1591,2018,2017,1628,1625,1562,2005)                    THEN "GRUPO 3 - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "2. Recorrente" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1582,1583,1624)                                                                                             THEN "GRUPO 3 - TESTE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "3. Bom Pag. D" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (0,186,204,220,258,270,1575,1581,1584,1588,1590,1591,1619,1621,2017,497,1625,2006)                      THEN "GRUPO 6 - BP DIGITAL - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "3. Bom Pag. D" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1627,1628)                                                                                                 THEN "GRUPO 6 - BP DIGITAL - TESTE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "4. Bom Pagado" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1,186,204,220,258,270,1619,2006,497,1625,1619,1583,1627,1628,1624,1625)                                                                       THEN "GRUPO 1 - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "4. Bom Pagado" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1575,1581,1584,1588,1590,1591,2017,1621)                                                                    THEN "GRUPO 1 - NOVA_REGUA"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "5. Outros PJ"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (186,204,258,270,497,1625,2006)                                                                         THEN "GRUPO 8 - OUTROS PJ - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "6. Outros PF"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1627,1628)                                                                                                  THEN "GRUPO 9 - OUTROS PF DIGITAL - TESTE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "6. Outros PF"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (0,186,204,220,258,270,1308,1562,1619,1625,2005,2006,497,1588,1575,1584,1591,2017)                                           THEN "GRUPO 9 - OUTROS PF DIGITAL - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "7. Outros PF"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (186,204,220,258,270,497,1309,1518,1562,1619,1625,2005,2006,2017,1590,1588,1621,1575,1581,1584,1591,1627,1628,1624)                               THEN "GRUPO 10 - OUTROS PF - CONTROLE"
		  ELSE "SEM GRUPO" END AS SEGMENTACAO_NEW,

PAGAMENTO_SITE,
CANAL

FROM ZNPS.BASE_NPS_ATU


WHERE 1=1
AND NOTA BETWEEN 0 AND 10
AND ENVIO NOT IN ('BNDU')


;QUIT;

proc contents data=ZNPS.BASE_NPS_ATU; run;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE AUX_NPS_1 AS

SELECT * FROM WORK.AUX_NPS_2021_COPIA

UNION ALL
SELECT * FROM AUX_NPS_NOVA

ORDER BY DIAREF

;RUN;

data AUX_NPS_1a;
	set AUX_NPS_NOVA;

	if atraso ge 60 and atraso  le 70 then fl_pre_cont=1;
	else fl_pre_cont=0;

	IF diaref <= MDY (08,23,2024) THEN DO;

	if DIVULGACAO='CARTAO_AMIGAVEL_DIG' and DIG_CPF_7 <= 4 then intervalor=1;
	else if DIVULGACAO='CARTAO_AMIGAVEL_DIG' then  intervalor=0;
	end;

	IF diaref > MDY (08,23,2024) THEN DO;

	if DIVULGACAO='CARTAO_AMIGAVEL_DIG' and DIG_CPF_7 <= 8 then intervalor=1;
	else if DIVULGACAO='CARTAO_AMIGAVEL_DIG' then  intervalor=0;
	end;

	if fl_pre_cont = 1 and DIG_CPF_7 < 2 then teste_pre_cont=1;
	else if fl_pre_cont = 1 then  teste_pre_cont=0;

run;

PROC SQL;
    ALTER TABLE AUX_NPS_NOVA
    DROP ENVIO_NORMALIZADO, PRODUTO, DIG_CPF_7;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE AUX_NPS AS
SELECT *,

CASE WHEN DIAREF BETWEEN '23APR2024'd AND '14MAY2024'd THEN 'ERRO ZENVIA'
ELSE 'ENVIO NORMAL' END AS FLAG_ERRO_ZENVIA,

CASE
WHEN ENVIO IN ('CARTAO') THEN 'CARTAO'
WHEN ENVIO IN ('CP') THEN 'CP'
ELSE 'RODAS' END AS ENVIO_NORMALIZADO

FROM WORK.AUX_NPS_NOVA

ORDER BY DIAREF

;RUN;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE MAX_DT_CONTRATO AS

MAX(DT_ENVIO) AS DT_ULTIMO_ENVIO FORMAT=DDMMYY10.,
NUM_CONTRATO

FROM ZNPS.ZENVIA_GERAL_ATU

GROUP BY
NUM_CONTRATO

;RUN;

PROC SQL;

CREATE TABLE SMS_UNIQUE AS

SELECT DISTINCT
A.*,
B.SMS

FROM WORK.MAX_DT_CONTRATO AS A
LEFT JOIN ZNPS.ZENVIA_GERAL_ATU AS B

ON A.NUM_CONTRATO = B.NUM_CONTRATO
AND A.DT_ULTIMO_ENVIO = B.DT_ENVIO

;RUN;

DATA WORK.SMS_UNIQUE;
SET SMS_UNIQUE;
	SMS2 = put(SMS,Z13.);
	DDD = 1*SUBSTR(SMS2,3,2)

;RUN;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE BASE_CLOSETHELOOP_FINAL AS

SELECT
A.NUCONTRATO,
A.NUCPF_CNPJ,
A.PERSONA_DETALHE,
A.idBV,
A.NOME,
C.TELEFONE_COMPLETO,
A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
A.QTD_PROMOTOR,
A.MAXDT_PROMOTOR,
A.QTD_NEUTRO,
A.MAXDT_NEUTRO,
A.QTD_DETRATOR,
A.MAXDT_DETRATOR,
A.QTD_SEMRESP,
A.MAXDT_SEMRESP,
A.DT_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA,
A.DT_ULTIMA_PESQUISA,
A.QTD_RESPOSTAS,
A.QTD_PESQUISAS,
A.FAIXA_DIAS_SEMRESP,
A.CONDENSADO_JORNADA,
A.JORNADA_NPS

FROM WORK.SINTETICO_JORNADA_FINAL AS A
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.COB_MDM_TELEFONE_BKPD3 AS C
ON A.idBV = C.idpessoa

WHERE A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO IN ('RODAS')

AND A.JORNADA_NPS IN ('DETRATOR','PROMOTOR > SEM RESPOSTA')

;RUN;

PROC SQL;

A.NUCONTRATO,

A.PERSONA_DETALHE,
A.idBV,

C.TELEFONE_COMPLETO,

A.MAXDT_SEMRESP,
A.DT_ULTIMA_RESPOSTA,

A.QTD_RESPOSTAS,

A.JORNADA_NPS

FROM WORK.SINTETICO_JORNADA_FINAL AS A
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.COB_MDM_TELEFONE_BKPD3 AS C
ON A.idBV = C.idpessoa

WHERE A.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO IN ('RODAS')

AND A.JORNADA_NPS IN ('DETRATOR','PROMOTOR > SEM RESPOSTA')

;RUN;

LIBNAME CONTATOS '/riscosbv/cobranca/rribeiro/contatos';

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE BASE_CLOSETHELOOP_FINAL AS

SELECT
A.*,

C.TELEFONE_COMPLETO,
case when put(A.NUCPF_CNPJ,z11.) = C.idpessoa then 1 else 0 end as fl_valida_tel

FROM WORK.AUX_NPS_NOVA AS A
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.cob_mdm_telefone AS C
ON put(A.NUCPF_CNPJ,z11.) = C.idpessoa

WHERE  A.SAFRA IN (202506)




and a.CATEGORIA_NPS='DETRATOR'

;

RUN;

  DBMS=csv
  REPLACE;
  PUTNAMES=YES;
 DELIMITER=';';

PROC SQL; 
CREATE TABLE AUX_NPS_NOVA AS
SELECT
NUCONTRATO,
NUCPF_CNPJ,
DIAREF,
SAFRA,
ENVIO,
CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW,
NMASSESSORIA,
DIVULGACAO,
NOTA,
CATEGORIA_NPS,
CASE WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('PROMOTOR')THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_PROMOTOR,
CASE WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('NEUTRO')THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_NEUTRO,
CASE WHEN CATEGORIA_NPS IN ('DETRATOR')THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_DETRATOR,
1 AS CONTADOR,

RESPOSTA_QUALITATIVA_CLI,
ATRASO,
FAIXA_ATRASO,
BV_COLABORA,
CLASSIFICACAO,
PUBLICOFINAL,
CASE
          
          WHEN ENVIO IN('BNDU') THEN "BNDU"
	      WHEN ENVIO IN('CARTAO') THEN "CARTAO"
	      WHEN ENVIO IN('CP') THEN "CP"
	      WHEN ENVIO IN('PILOTO CRC') THEN "PILOTO CRC"
	      WHEN ENVIO IN('PILOTO ACORDO CERTO') THEN "PILOTO ACORDO CERTO"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "VIC"          AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2019,1575,1581,1584,1588,1589,1590,1591,1621) THEN "VIC - TESTE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "VIC"          AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2006,1522,1580,1518,1601,2005,1562,1541,1578,186,204,220,241,258,270,497) THEN "VIC - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 2"      AND PUBLICOFINAL = "Teste" THEN "GRUPO 2 - TESTE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 2"      AND PUBLICOFINAL = "Controle" THEN "GRUPO 2 - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 1"      AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2017,2018,1575,1581,1584,1588,1589,1590,1591,1621) THEN "GRUPO 1 - NOVA_REGUA"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 1"      AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2006,1522,1580,1518,1601,2005,1562,1541,1578,186,204,220,241,258,270,497) THEN "GRUPO 1 - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 3"      AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1582,1583) THEN "GRUPO 3 - TESTE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 3"      AND PUBLICOFINAL = "Teste" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2005,1562,2006) THEN "GRUPO 3 - TESTE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Grupo 3"      AND PUBLICOFINAL = "Controle" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2005,1562,2006,1541,1578,186,204,220,241,258,270,497) THEN "GRUPO 3 - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Prop Media"   AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2020,1575,1581,1584,1588,1589,1590,1591,1621) THEN "GRUPO 1a4 - TESTE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "Prop Media"   AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (2006,1522,1580,1518,1601,2005,1562,1541,1578,186,204,220,241,258,270,497) THEN "GRUPO 1a4 - CONTROLE"

          
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "1. Prim. PMT"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (186,204,220,258,270,497,1309,1562,1590,1592,1619,1625,2005,2017,1588,1575,1591,1627,2006)                               THEN "GRUPO 7 - PRI_PMT - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "2. Recorrente" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (186,204,220,258,270,497,1306,1518,1562,1619,1625,1627,2005,2006,2017,1628,1590,1588,1621,1575,1581,1584,1591,1628,220,258,1619,497,204,270,186,1590,1575,1591,2018,2017,1628,1625,1562,2005)                    THEN "GRUPO 3 - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "2. Recorrente" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1582,1583,1624)                                                                                             THEN "GRUPO 3 - TESTE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "3. Bom Pag. D" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (0,186,204,220,258,270,1575,1581,1584,1588,1590,1591,1619,1621,2017,497,1625,2006)                      THEN "GRUPO 6 - BP DIGITAL - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "3. Bom Pag. D" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1627,1628)                                                                                                 THEN "GRUPO 6 - BP DIGITAL - TESTE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "4. Bom Pagado" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1,186,204,220,258,270,1619,2006,497,1625,1619,1583,1627,1628,1624,1625)                                                                       THEN "GRUPO 1 - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "4. Bom Pagado" AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1575,1581,1584,1588,1590,1591,2017,1621)                                                                    THEN "GRUPO 1 - NOVA_REGUA"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "5. Outros PJ"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (186,204,258,270,497,1625,2006)                                                                         THEN "GRUPO 8 - OUTROS PJ - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "6. Outros PF"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (1627,1628)                                                                                                  THEN "GRUPO 9 - OUTROS PF DIGITAL - TESTE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "6. Outros PF"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (0,186,204,220,258,270,1308,1562,1619,1625,2005,2006,497,1588,1575,1584,1591,2017)                                           THEN "GRUPO 9 - OUTROS PF DIGITAL - CONTROLE"
          WHEN CLASSIFICACAO = "7. Outros PF"  AND CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW IN (186,204,220,258,270,497,1309,1518,1562,1619,1625,2005,2006,2017,1590,1588,1621,1575,1581,1584,1591,1627,1628,1624)                               THEN "GRUPO 10 - OUTROS PF - CONTROLE"
		  ELSE "SEM GRUPO" END AS SEGMENTACAO_NEW,

PAGAMENTO_SITE,
CANAL,
case when CANAL='ASSESSORIA' or CANAL='MANUAL ASSESSORIA' then 'ASSESSORIA'
when CANAL ne '' then 'DIGITAL'
else 'CARTAO' end as CANAL_MACRO,

CASE
WHEN NOTA BETWEEN 0 AND 10 THEN 'RESPONDEU'
ELSE 'NÃO RESPONDEU' END AS FLAG_RESPOSTA

FROM ZNPS.BASE_NPS_ATU


WHERE 1=1
AND ENVIO NOT IN ('BNDU')


;QUIT;

data TOTAL_NPS_RODAS_2;
	set total_nps_rodas(where=(DT_ENVIO<>'DT_ENVIO' and DT_ENVIO <> ''));
	DT_REF = input(DT_ENVIO, ddmmyy10.);

	format DT_REF ddmmyy10.;

run;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_AUX_NPS1 AS
   SELECT t1.CATEGORIA_NPS,
          t1.NMASSESSORIA,

          t1.ENVIO,

               t1.NMASSESSORIA,

               t1.SAFRA;

PROC SQL ;
CONNECT TO BIGQUERY AS PERM (
project   = 'bv-sadi-dtm'
credfile  = '/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json'
schema    = 'bv-pdbd-prd'
Read_Mode = Storage
Allow_Large_Results = must
Max_Char_Len = 40
);
CREATE TABLE WORK.MODELO_PROP_DIG AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.*
, CASE WHEN GH_dvry >7 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_PROP_DIG
, NUCONTRATO FORMAT= BEST14. INFORMAT=best14. AS NUCONTRATOGESTAO
, GH_dvry AS GH
FROM CONNECTION TO PERM (
SELECT DISTINCT *
from bv-pdbd-prd.dvry_anly_cienciadadocobr.tbPropensaoPagamentoDigital
where particao in (202502,202503)) AS A
;DISCONNECT FROM PERM
;QUIT;

proc sql ;
connect to bigquery as perm (project='bv-sadi-dtm' credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json'
      schema='bv-pdbd-prd'
READ_MODE	= storage
ALLOW_LARGE_RESULTS = must
MAX_CHAR_LEN = 20
);
   Create table COLL_5_30_202503 as
   select * ,
	CASE
	WHEN GH IN ('1','2','3','4',) 					THEN 'RUIM'
	WHEN GH IN ('01','02','03','04',) 				THEN 'RUIM'
	WHEN GH IN ('5','6','7','8','9') 				THEN 'REGULAR'
	WHEN GH IN ('05','06','07','08','09') 			THEN 'REGULAR'
	WHEN GH IN ('10','11') 							THEN 'REGULAR'
	WHEN GH IN ('12','13','14','15','16','17','18') THEN 'BOM'
	ELSE GH END AS COLLECTION
      from connection to perm
     (select  * from bv-pdbd-prd.dvry_anly_cienciadadocobr.tbmodelcollectionsleves5_30d where safra=date(2025,02,01));
   disconnect from perm;
quit;

proc sql ;

      schema='bv-pdbd-prd'
READ_MODE	= storage
ALLOW_LARGE_RESULTS = must
MAX_CHAR_LEN = 20
);
   Create table COLL_5_30_202502 as

	WHEN GH IN ('1','2','3','4',) 					THEN 'RUIM'
	WHEN GH IN ('01','02','03','04',) 				THEN 'RUIM'

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE ERRO_ATIVA_NOV24_SDUP_COLL AS
SELECT
	A.*
	,b.COLLECTION,
case when A.nr_contrato = B.nucontrato then 1 else 0 end as flag_coll
FROM WORK.ERRO_ATIVA_NOV24_SDUP AS A
LEFT JOIN WORK.COLL_5_30_202410	AS B ON A.nr_contrato = B.nucontrato
;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE AUX_NPS_02 AS
SELECT
	A.*
	,b.*,
case when DIVULGACAO in ('ATIVA_BOM_PAGADOR') then 'ATIVA - BP'
WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('ATIVA_BOM_PAGADOR_DIGITAL',
'ATIVA_TRADICIONAL', 'ATIVA_RECORRENTE',
'ATIVA_DEMAISPF_DIGITAL', 'ATIVA_VISAO_UNICA') then 'ATIVA - DEMAIS'
WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('ADMINISTRATIVA_BOMPAGADOR') THEN 'ADM - BP'
WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('ADM_DEMAISPF_DIGITAL', 'ADMINISTRATIVA', 'ADMINISTRATIVA_RECORRENTE',
'ADMINISTRATIVA_RENEG') THEN 'ADM - DEMAIS'
WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('CARTAO_AMIGAVEL') THEN 'CA'
WHEN DIVULGACAO in ('CARTAO_AMIGAVEL_DIG') THEN 'CA - DIG'
ELSE  'OUTROS(CP,SOLAR,CONSIG)'
END AS DIVULG_AGRUP,

case when CANAL='ASSESSORIA' or CANAL='MANUAL ASSESSORIA' then 'ASSESSORIA'
when CANAL ne '' then 'DIGITAL'
else 'CARTAO' end as CANAL_MACRO,

case when A.NUCONTRATO = B.NR_CONTRATO then 1 else 0 end as FLAG_ERRO_ATIVA
FROM WORK.AUX_NPS AS A
LEFT JOIN WORK.ERRO_ATIVA_NOV24_SDUP_COLL	AS B ON A.NUCONTRATO = B.NR_CONTRATO
;QUIT;

data AUX_NPS_03;
	set AUX_NPS_02;
	if FLAG_ERRO_ATIVA=1 and collection='BOM'
and DIVULG_AGRUP not in ('ATIVA - BP' 'ADM - BP') then FL_BP_ERRO=1;
else  FL_BP_ERRO=0;
run;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_AUX_NPS1 AS
   SELECT t1.CATEGORIA_NPS,
          t1.FL_BP_ERRO,
          t1.FAIXA_ATRASO,
          t1.CANAL_MACRO,
          t1.RESPOSTA_QUALITATIVA_CLI,
          t1.DIVULG_AGRUP,
          t1.FLAG_ERRO_ATIVA,
          t1.SAFRA,
          t1.FLAG_RESPOSTA,
		  t1.COLLECTION,

          
            (COUNT(t1.NUCONTRATO)) AS COUNT_of_NUCONTRATO,
          
            (SUM(t1.FLAG_PROMOTOR)) FORMAT=BEST1. AS SUM_of_FLAG_PROMOTOR,
          
            (SUM(t1.FLAG_NEUTRO)) FORMAT=BEST1. AS SUM_of_FLAG_NEUTRO,
          
            (SUM(t1.FLAG_DETRATOR)) FORMAT=BEST1. AS SUM_of_FLAG_DETRATOR
      FROM WORK.AUX_NPS_03 t1
	  where t1.SAFRA ge 202401
      GROUP BY t1.CATEGORIA_NPS,
               t1.FL_BP_ERRO,
               t1.FAIXA_ATRASO,
               t1.CANAL_MACRO,
               t1.RESPOSTA_QUALITATIVA_CLI,
               t1.DIVULG_AGRUP,
               t1.FLAG_ERRO_ATIVA,
               t1.SAFRA,
               t1.FLAG_RESPOSTA,
		  	   t1.COLLECTION
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_AUX_NPS1 AS
   SELECT t1.CATEGORIA_NPS,
          t1.FL_BP_ERRO,
          t1.FAIXA_ATRASO,
          t1.CANAL_MACRO,
          t1.RESPOSTA_QUALITATIVA_CLI,
          t1.DIVULG_AGRUP,
          t1.FLAG_ERRO_ATIVA,
          t1.SAFRA,
          t1.FLAG_RESPOSTA,
		  t1.NMASSESSORIA,

          
            (COUNT(t1.NUCONTRATO)) AS COUNT_of_NUCONTRATO,
          
            (SUM(t1.FLAG_PROMOTOR)) FORMAT=BEST1. AS SUM_of_FLAG_PROMOTOR,
          
            (SUM(t1.FLAG_NEUTRO)) FORMAT=BEST1. AS SUM_of_FLAG_NEUTRO,
          
            (SUM(t1.FLAG_DETRATOR)) FORMAT=BEST1. AS SUM_of_FLAG_DETRATOR
      FROM WORK.AUX_NPS_03 t1
	  where t1.SAFRA ge 202401
      GROUP BY t1.CATEGORIA_NPS,
               t1.FL_BP_ERRO,
               t1.FAIXA_ATRASO,
               t1.CANAL_MACRO,
               t1.RESPOSTA_QUALITATIVA_CLI,
               t1.DIVULG_AGRUP,
               t1.FLAG_ERRO_ATIVA,
               t1.SAFRA,
               t1.FLAG_RESPOSTA,
		  	   t1.NMASSESSORIA
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CONNECT TO SYBASE (SERVER=PTASYBCTR DATABASE=DBCOR USER= 'alessandra_cruz' PASSWORD= '*G7ENXAY#I+C');

CREATE TABLE WORK.SIMULADOR_RENEG AS
SELECT *

FROM CONNECTION TO SYBASE(

    SELECT DISTINCT
        NuContratoGestao,
        SqContratoFinanceiro,
        NuContratoGestaoNovo,
        CASE WHEN NuContratoGestao = NuContratoGestaoNovo THEN SqContratoFinanceiro+1 ELSE 1 END AS SQCONTRATONOVO,
        CASE
                WHEN St_Reneg = 1 THEN 'Simulado'
                WHEN St_Reneg = 2 THEN 'Liberado'
                WHEN St_Reneg = 4 THEN 'Pendente'
                WHEN St_Reneg = 3 THEN 'Implantado'
                END AS Ds_Reneg,

        CASE WHEN Dt_Cancelamento IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1
                END AS Fl_Cancelamento,
        St_Reneg,
        dt_baixa,
        dt_reneg,
        vr_presente,
        vr_financiado,
        qt_parcela,
        vr_prestacao,
        vr_desconto_mora,
        vr_desconto_multa,
        vr_desconto_principal,
        Tx_Pactuada

        FROM dbreneg..tb_reneg_simulador



)
;QUIT;

from connection to dbcon (select

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE DETRATORES_2_202504 AS
SELECT
	A.*,
	CASE
WHEN a.ENVIO IN ('CARTAO') THEN 'CARTAO'
WHEN a.ENVIO IN ('CP') THEN 'CP'
ELSE 'RODAS' END AS ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,

case when A.NUCONTRATO = B.NUCONTRATO then 1 else 0 end as flag_det_abr25
FROM WORK.AUX_NPS_NOVA_3 AS A
LEFT JOIN WORK.DETRATORES_202504 AS B ON A.NUCONTRATO = B.NUCONTRATO
;QUIT;

data DETRATORES_AUX;
	set DETRATORES_2_202504(where=(safra ne 202504 and flag_det_abr25=1)) ;
run;

	BY NUCONTRATO DESCENDING DIAREF;

   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY3_0000 AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.DIVULG_AGRUP,
          t1.NMASSESSORIA2 as NMASSESSORIA,
          t1.CANAL_MACRO,
          t1.RESPOSTA_QUALITATIVA_CLI,
          t1.DU,
          t1.FAIXA_ATRASO,
          t1.SEGMENTACAO_NEW,
		  t1.CATEGORIA_NPS,
		  t1.CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW,
		  t1.DIVULGACAO,
		  t1.flag_det_abr25,
		  t1.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
          t1.FLAG_RESPOSTA,
          
            (COUNT(t1.NUCONTRATO)) AS COUNT_of_NUCONTRATO,
          
            (SUM(t1.FLAG_PROMOTOR)) AS SUM_of_FLAG_PROMOTOR,
          
            (SUM(t1.FLAG_NEUTRO)) AS SUM_of_FLAG_NEUTRO,
          
            (SUM(t1.FLAG_DETRATOR)) AS SUM_of_FLAG_DETRATOR,
          
            (SUM(t1.CONTADOR)) AS SUM_of_CONTADOR
      FROM WORK.DETRATORES_2_202504 t1
      GROUP BY t1.SAFRA,
               t1.DIVULG_AGRUP,
               t1.NMASSESSORIA2,
               t1.CANAL_MACRO,
               t1.RESPOSTA_QUALITATIVA_CLI,
               t1.DU,
               t1.FAIXA_ATRASO,
               t1.SEGMENTACAO_NEW,
		  t1.CATEGORIA_NPS,
		  t1.CD_ASSESSORIA_CONSIDERAR_NEW,
		  t1.DIVULGACAO,
		  t1.flag_det_abr25,
		  t1.ENVIO_NORMALIZADO,
               t1.FLAG_RESPOSTA;
QUIT;

%MACRO HISTORICO_GRAVACAO (MES_AUX, OBSERV=MAX);


%let _timer_start = %sysfunc(datetime());

OPTIONS OBS= &OBSERV;

PROC SQL;
CONNECT TO sybaseiq AS dbcon
(host='pta-iqprd01' server=ptaiqprd db=PTAIQCONT user=&user. password=&passwd.);
CREATE TABLE PUBLICO_POTENCIAL_N7 AS
SELECT	A.*,
		B.NuScore AS Score3_Historico,
		B.DtGravacaoScore AS DtGravacaoHist
from	PUBLICO_POTENCIAL_N6 AS A
		LEFT JOIN CONNECTION TO dbcon
		(SELECT NuContratoGestao,
				SqContratoFinanceiro,
				MAX(DtGravacaoScore) AS DtGravacaoScore
		INTO	#BASE1
		FROM	DBCOBRANCA.TbScoreCobranca
		WHERE	CdModelo = 78
		GROUP BY NuContratoGestao, SqContratoFinanceiro

		SELECT	A.NuContratoGestao,
				A.SqContratoFinanceiro,
				DATE(A.DtGravacaoScore) AS DtGravacaoScore,
				B.DsAcaoCobranca,
				B.NuScore
		FROM	#BASE1 AS A
				LEFT JOIN DBCOBRANCA.TbScoreCobranca AS B ON	A.NuContratoGestao = B.NuContratoGestao AND
																A.SqContratoFinanceiro = B.SqContratoFinanceiro AND
																A.DtGravacaoScore = B.DtGravacaoScore AND
																B.CdModelo = 78) AS B
ON	A.NuContratoGestao = B.NuContratoGestao AND
	A.SqContratoFinanceiro = B.SqContratoFinanceiro;
QUIT;

OPTIONS OBS= MAX;


data _NULL_;
  dur = datetime() - &_timer_start;
  put 30*'-' / ' Total Duration:' dur time13.2 / 30*'-';
run;

%MEND HISTORICO_GRAVACAO;

%HISTORICO_GRAVACAO (OBSERV=MAX);

PROC SQL ;
CONNECT TO BIGQUERY AS PERM (
project   = 'bv-sadi-dtm'
credfile  = '/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json'
schema    = 'bv-pdbd-prd'
Read_Mode = Storage
Allow_Large_Results = must
Max_Char_Len = 60
);
CREATE TABLE WORK.TB_BOLETOSITE_IQ AS
SELECT DISTINCT

				C.TPENTRADA,
				C.NUPROCESSO,
				C.NUCONTRATOGESTAO AS NUCONTRATOGESTAO FORMAT BEST14.,
				C.SQCONTRATOFINANCEIRO,
				C.DTGERACAO,
				C.DTPAGAMENTO,
				C.DTVENCIMENTO,
				C.CANAL,
			    C.RAZAO_NOME,
			    C.COD_ACAO,
			    C.DT_ENVIO,
			    C.DT_RETIRADA,
			    C.CDAGENTERECEBEDOR,
			    C.NMAGENTERECEBEDOR

FROM CONNECTION TO PERM (

WITH BASE_GERACAO AS (

			 SELECT

					B.NUCONTRATOGESTAO,
					B.SQCONTRATOFINANCEIRO,
					B.DTGERACAO

			FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_dbcontencioso.tbnegociacaocontrato B
			WHERE
					CAST(DTGERACAO AS DATE)>='2026-01-28' AND CAST(DTGERACAO AS DATE)<='2026-02-28' 

		),
BASE_PAGAMENTO AS (
			SELECT

				b.TPENTRADA,
				cast(b.NUPROCESSO AS NUMERIC) AS NUPROCESSO,
				CAST(A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO AS NUMERIC) AS NUCONTRATOGESTAO,
				A.SQCONTRATOFINANCEIRO,
				A.DTGERACAO,
				B.DTPAGAMENTO,
				B.DTVENCIMENTO

			FROM BASE_GERACAO A LEFT JOIN bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_dbcontencioso.tbnegociacaocontrato B
			ON
				A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO=B.NUCONTRATOGESTAO
			AND
				A.SQCONTRATOFINANCEIRO=b.SQCONTRATOFINANCEIRO
			AND
				A.DTGERACAO = B.DTGERACAO

			WHERE
				B.DTPAGAMENTO IS NOT NULL
			AND
				CAST(B.DtPagamento AS DATE) >= '2026-01-28' AND CAST(B.DtPagamento AS DATE) <= '2026-02-28'
			AND
				B.STNEGOCIACAO='PL')

			SELECT

				   A.*,
										CASE
						 WHEN TpEntrada='A' THEN 'ASSESSORIA'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='R' THEN 'ROBO'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='B' THEN 'CHATBOT'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='S' THEN 'SITE'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='C' THEN 'SAC'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='U' THEN 'URA'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='H' THEN 'APP'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='F' THEN 'MKTPLACE SERASA'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='G' THEN 'MKTPLACE QUERO QUITAR'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='D' THEN 'MKTPLACE BLUE365'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='E' THEN 'MKTPLACE ACORDO CERTO'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='I' THEN 'ROBBU'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='J' THEN 'RECALIBRA'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='Q' THEN 'WHATSAPP BOT'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='M' THEN 'MANUAL ASSESSORIA'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='L' THEN 'WHATSAPP SF SAC'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='T' THEN 'WHATSAPP SF COB'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='V' THEN 'JABUTI'
						 WHEN TpEntrada='O' THEN 'GOOGLE'
                         ELSE 'OUTROS' END AS CANAL,
				   E.RAZAO_NOME,
				   B.COD_ACAO,
				   CAST(B.DT_ASSES AS DATE) AS DT_ENVIO,
				   CAST(B.DT_RET_ASSES AS DATE) AS DT_RETIRADA,
				   G.CDAGENTERECEBEDOR,
				   G.NMAGENTERECEBEDOR

				FROM BASE_PAGAMENTO A LEFT JOIN bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_scc_fin.saj_processo B
				ON
				A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO= CAST(B.NUCONTRATOGESTAO AS NUMERIC)
				AND
				A.SQCONTRATOFINANCEIRO=B.SQCONTRATOFINANCEIRO
				AND
				CAST(A.NUPROCESSO AS INTEGER)=B.NUM_PROCESSO

				LEFT JOIN bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_scc_fin.saj_agente E
				ON
				B.COD_ASSES=E.COD_AGENTE

				LEFT JOIN bv-pdbd-prd.dvry_core.tbparcelarecebimento F
				on A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO= CAST(F.CD_CONTRATO AS NUMERIC)
				AND
				A.SQCONTRATOFINANCEIRO=F.SQ_CONTRATO_FINANCEIRO AND CAST(A.DTPAGAMENTO AS DATE) = CAST(F.Dt_RECEBIMENTO AS DATE)

				LEFT JOIN bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_dbcor.tbagenterecebedor G
				ON cast(F.CD_AGENTE_RECEBEDOR as numeric) =G.CDAGENTERECEBEDOR

) AS C

;DISCONNECT FROM PERM
;QUIT;

proc sort data=TB_BOLETOSITE_IQ;
	by NUCONTRATOGESTAO descending SQCONTRATOFINANCEIRO descending DTPAGAMENTO;
run;

proc sort data=TB_BOLETOSITE_IQ nodupkey out= TB_BOLETOSITE_SDUP dupout=DUPS_BOLETO;
	by NUCONTRATOGESTAO;
run;

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE BASE_SAIDA AS
SELECT
	A.*
	,b.*,
case when A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO = B.NUCONTRATOGESTAO  AND A.SqContratoFinanceiro = B.SqContratoFinanceiro then 1 else 0 end as flag
FROM WORK.BASE_A AS A
LEFT JOIN WORK.BASE_B	AS B ON A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO = B.NUCONTRATOGESTAO  AND A.SqContratoFinanceiro = B.SqContratoFinanceiro
;QUIT;
